SessionState uint\n47\t\n48\tconst (\n49\t\tDefaultView SessionState = iota\n50\t\tLatestView\n51\t\tCategoriesView\n52\t\tCategoryProductsView\n53\t\tProductView\n54\t)\n55\t\n56\ttype MainModel struct {\n57\t\tState                 SessionState\n58\t\tCurrentView           tea.Model\n59\t\tPreviousViews         []tea.Model // Stack to store previous views for back navigation\n60\t\tDefaultModel          defaultview.DefaultModel\n61\t\tLatestModel           latest.LatestModel\n62\t\tCategoriesModel       categories.Model\n63\t\tCategoryProductsModel categoryproducts.Model\n64\t\tProductModel          productview.ProductModel\n65\t\tLoading               bool\n66\t\tSpinner               spinner.Model\n67\t\tWidth                 int\n68\t\tHeight                int\n69\t\tErrMsg                string // To store and display error messages\n70\t}\n71\t\n72\tfunc InitialModel() MainModel {\n73\t\ts := spinner.New()\n74\t\ts.Spinner = spinner.Dot\n75\t\ts.Style = lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"205\"))\n76\t\n77\t\tdm := defaultview.InitialModel()\n78\t\tlm := latest.NewLatestModel()\n79\t\tcm := categories.NewCategoriesModel()\n80\t\t// For cpm, categoryName and categoryID will be updated when a category is chosen.\n81\t\t// Initialize with placeholder values.\n82\t\tcpm := DefaultView,\n87\t\t\tCurrentView:           dm,\n88\t\t\tDefaultModel:          dm,\n89\t\t\tLatestModel:           lm,\n90\t\t\tCategoriesModel:       cm,\n91\t\t\tCategoryProductsModel: cpm,\n92\t\t\tProductModel:          pm,\n93\t\t\tPreviousViews:         []tea.Model{},\n94\t\t\tLoading:               false,\n95\t\t\tSpinner:               s,\n96\t\t\t// Width and Height will be set by tea.WindowSizeMsg\n97\t\t}\n98\t}\n99\t\n100\tfunc (mm MainModel) Init() tea.Cmd {\n101\t\treturn mm.Spinner.Tick\n102\t}\n103\t\n104\tfunc (mm MainModel) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n105\t\tvar cmd tea.Cmd\n106\t\tvar cmds []tea.Cmd\n107\t\tvar updatedViewModel tea.Model\n108\t\n109\t\tswitch msg := msg.(type) {\n110\t\tcase tea.WindowSizeMsg:\n111\t\t\tmm.Width = msg.Width\n112\t\t\tmm.Height = msg.Height\n113\t\tcase tea.KeyMsg:\n114\t\t\tswitch msg.String() {\n115\t\t\tcase \"q\":\n116\t\t\t\treturn mm, tea.Quit\n117\t\t\tcase \"h\", \"left\":\n118\t\t\t\tif len(mm.PreviousViews) > 0 {\n119\t\t\t\t\tlastViewIndex := len(mm.PreviousViews) - 1\n120\t\t\t\t\tmm.CurrentView = mm.PreviousViews[lastViewIndex]\n121\t\t\t\t\tmm.PreviousViews = mm.PreviousViews[:lastViewIndex]\n122\t\n123\t\t\t\t\tswitch mm.CurrentView.(type) {\n124\t\t\t\t\tcase defaultview.DefaultModel:\n125\t\t\t\t\t\tmm.State = DefaultView\n126\t\t\t\t\tcase latest.LatestModel:\n127\t\t\t\t\t\tmm.State = LatestView\n128\t\t\t\t\tcase categories.Model:\n129\t\t\t\t\t\tmm.State = CategoriesView\n130\t\t\t\t\tcase categoryproducts.Model:\n131\t\t\t\t\t\tmm.State = CategoryProductsView\n132\t\t\t\t\tcase productview.ProductModel:\n133\t\t\t\t\t\tmm.State = ProductView\n134\t\t\t\t\t}\n135\t\t\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n136\t\t\t\t}\n137\t\t\t}\n138\t\tcase defaultview.StartLoadingLatestMsg:\n139\t\t\tmm.Loading = true\n140\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n141\t\t\t// Ensure initial load is page 1, use PerPage from the model (defaulted in NewLatestModel)\n142\t\t\tcmds = append(cmds, commands.HandleGetLatest(mm.Width, mm.Height, 1, mm.LatestModel.PerPage))\n143\t\n144\t\tcase defaultview.StartLoadingCategoriesMsg:\n145\t\t\tmm.Loading = true\n146\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n147\t\t\tcmds = append(cmds, commands.HandleGetCategories(mm.Width, mm.Height))\n148\t\n149\t\tcase commands.LatestResponseMsg:\n150\t\t\tmm.Loading = false\n151\t\t\tif msg.Err != nil {\n152\t\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = msg.Err.Error()\n153\t\t\t} else {\n154\t\t\t\t// If not already in LatestView, it's an initial load for this view type.\n155\t\t\t\tif mm.State != LatestView {\n156\t\t\t\t\t// mm.LatestModel = latest.NewLatestModel() // Already initialized in InitialModel\n157\t\t\t\t\tmm.PreviousViews = append(mm.PreviousViews, mm.CurrentView) // Only add to stack if changing view type\n158\t\t\t\t\tmm.State = LatestView\n159\t\t\t\t}\n160\t\t\t\t// Update the existing (or newly created) LatestModel instance\n161\t\t\t\tupdatedViewModel, cmd = mm.LatestModel.Update(msg)\n162\t\t\t\tmm.LatestModel = updatedViewModel.(latest.LatestModel)\n163\t\t\t\tcmds = append(cmds, cmd)\n164\t\t\t\tmm.CurrentView = mm.LatestModel // Ensure CurrentView points to the updated model\n165\t\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n166\t\t\t}\n167\t\n168\t\tcase commands.LoadLatestPageMsg: // New\n169\t\t\tmm.Loading = true\n170\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n171\t\t\t// The CurrentView is still LatestModel, so we don't push to PreviousViews\n172\t\t\t// We are just re-loading data for the current view type.\n173\t\t\tcmds = append(cmds, commands.HandleGetLatest(mm.Width, mm.Height, msg.Page, msg.PerPage))\n174\t\n175\t\tcase commands.CategoriesResponseMsg:\n176\t\t\tmm.Loading = false\n177\t\t\tif msg.Err != nil {\n178\t\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = msg.Err.Error()\n179\t\t\t} else {\n180\t\t\t\tupdatedViewModel, cmd = mm.CategoriesModel.Update(msg)\n181\t\t\t\tmm.CategoriesModel = updatedViewModel.(categories.Model)\n182\t\t\t\tcmds = append(cmds, cmd)\n183\t\t\t\tmm.PreviousViews = append(mm.PreviousViews, mm.CurrentView)\n184\t\t\t\tmm.CurrentView = mm.CategoriesModel\n185\t\t\t\tmm.State = CategoriesView\n186\t\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n187\t\t\t}\n188\t\n189\t\tcase commands.StartLoadingProductsForCategoryMsg:\n190\t\t\tmm.Loading = true\n191\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n192\t\t\t// Ensure initial load is page 1, use PerPage from the model (defaulted in NewCategoryProductsModel)\n193\t\t\tcmds = append(cmds, commands.HandleGetProductsByCategory(mm.Width, mm.Height, msg.CategoryID, msg.CategoryName, msg.APIEndpoint, 1, mm.CategoryProductsModel.PerPage))\n194\t\n195\t\tcase commands.ProductsForCategoryResponseMsg:\n196\t\t\tmm.Loading = false\n197\t\t\tif msg.Err != nil {\n198\t\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = msg.Err.Error()\n199\t\t\t} else {\n200\t\t\t\t// If current state is not CategoryProductsView OR if the category ID differs,\n201\t\t\t\t// it's a new category product listing.\n202\t\t\t\tif mm.State != CategoryProductsView || mm.CategoryProductsModel.CategoryID() != msg.CategoryID { // Used getter\n203\t\t\t\t\tmm.CategoryProductsModel = categoryproducts.NewModel(msg.CategoryName, msg.CategoryID, msg.APIEndpoint)\n204\t\t\t\t\tmm.PreviousViews = append(mm.PreviousViews, mm.CurrentView)\n205\t\t\t\t\tmm.State = CategoryProductsView\n206\t\t\t\t}\n207\t\t\t\t// Update the model (either newly created or existing)\n208\t\t\t\tupdatedViewModel, cmd := mm.CategoryProductsModel.Update(msg)\n209\t\t\t\tmm.CategoryProductsModel = updatedViewModel.(categoryproducts.Model)\n210\t\t\t\tcmds = append(cmds, cmd)\n211\t\t\t\tmm.CurrentView = mm.CategoryProductsModel\n212\t\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n213\t\t\t}\n214\t\n215\t\tcase commands.LoadCategoryProductsPageMsg: // New\n216\t\t\tmm.Loading = true\n217\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n218\t\t\t// Similar to LoadLatestPageMsg, CurrentView is CategoryProductsModel.\n219\t\t\tcmds = append(cmds, commands.HandleGetProductsByCategory(mm.Width, mm.Height, msg.CategoryID, msg.CategoryName, msg.APIEndpoint, msg.Page, msg.PerPage))\n220\t\n221\t\tcase commands.ProductsMsg: // This is for displaying a single product\n222\t\t\tmm.Loading = false\n223\t\t\tif msg.Err != nil {\n224\t\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = msg.Err.Error()\n225\t\t\t} else {\n226\t\t\t\tupdatedViewModel, cmd = mm.ProductModel.Update(msg)\n227\t\t\t\tmm.ProductModel = updatedViewModel.(productview.ProductModel)\n228\t\t\t\tcmds = append(cmds, cmd)\n229\t\t\t\tmm.PreviousViews = append(mm.PreviousViews, mm.CurrentView)\n230\t\t\t\tmm.CurrentView = mm.ProductModel\n231\t\t\t\tmm.State = ProductView\n232\t\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n233\t\t\t}\n234\t\t}\n235\t\n236\t\t// Update current view & spinner if loading\n237\t\t// Only pass non-nil messages or messages the current view specifically handles\n238\t\t// This avoids passing spinner ticks or other messages to views not expecting them if not loading\n239\t\tif !mm.Loading {\n240\t\t\tvar currentViewCmd tea.Cmd\n241\t\t\tmm.CurrentView, currentViewCmd = mm.CurrentView.Update(msg)\n242\t\t\tcmds = append(cmds, currentViewCmd)\n243\t\t}\n244\t\n245\t\tif mm.Loading {\n246\t\t\tvar spinCmd tea.Cmd\n247\t\t\tmm.Spinner, spinCmd = mm.Spinner.Update(msg) // Spinner needs ticks\n248\t\t\tcmds = append(cmds, spinCmd)\n249\t\t} else {\n250\t\t\t// If not loading, ensure current view is updated even if no specific msg type matched above\n251\t\t\t// This handles general key presses for list navigation within the current view\n252\t\t\tif _, ok := msg.(tea.KeyMsg); ok { // Only pass key messages if not loading and not handled above\n253\t\t\t\tvar currentViewCmd tea.Cmd\n254\t\t\t\tmm.CurrentView, currentViewCmd = mm.CurrentView.Update(msg)\n255\t\t\t\tcmds = append(cmds, currentViewCmd)\n256\t\t\t}\n257\t\t}\n258\t\n259\t\treturn mm, tea.Batch(cmds...)\n260\t}\n261\t\n262\tfunc (mm MainModel) View() string {\n263\t\t// var mainViewContent string // Removed as it was declared and not used before re-assignment\n264\t\tvar helpContent string\n265\t\n266\t\tswitch mm.State {\n267\t\tcase DefaultView:\n268\t\t\thelpContent = \"↑/↓: navigate | enter: select | q: quit\"\n269\t\tcase LatestView, CategoryProductsView:\n270\t\t\thelpContent = \"↑/↓: navigate | enter: select | h/←: back | n: next | p: prev | q: quit\"\n271\t\tcase CategoriesView: // Categories view does not have n/p for its own list\n272\t\t\thelpContent = \"↑/↓: navigate | enter: select | h/←: back | q: quit\"\n273\t\tcase ProductView:\n274\t\t\thelpContent = \"h/←: back | q: quit\"\n275\t\tdefault:\n276\t\t\thelpContent = \"q: quit\"\n277\t\t}\n278\t\n279\t\tif mm.ErrMsg != \"\" {\n280\t\t\thelpContent = \"h/←: back | q: quit\"\n281\t\t}\n282\t\n283\t\tfooterStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().\n284\t\t\tBorder(lipgloss.NormalBorder(), true, false, false, false).\n285\t\t\tPaddingTop(1).\n286\t\t\tForeground(lipgloss.Color(\"240\"))\n287\t\n288\t\tstyledFooter := footerStyle.Width(mm.Width).Render(helpContent)\n289\t\n290\t\tif mm.ErrMsg != \"\" {\n291\t\t\terrorStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"#FF0000\")).Width(mm.Width).Align(lipgloss.Center)\n292\t\t\terrorMessage := errorStyle.Render(\"Error: \" + mm.ErrMsg)\n293\t\t\terrorViewHeight := max(mm.Height-lipgloss.Height(styledFooter), 0)\n294\t\t\tcenteredError := lipgloss.Place(mm.Width, errorViewHeight, lipgloss.Center, lipgloss.Center, errorMessage)\n295\t\t\treturn lipgloss.JoinVertical(lipgloss.Left, centeredError, styledFooter)\n296\t\t}\n297\t\n298\t\tif mm.Loading {\n299\t\t\tloadingViewContent := lipgloss.JoinVertical(\n300\t\t\t\tlipgloss.Center,\n301\t\t\t\tlogo,\n302\t\t\t\tmm.Spinner.View(),\n303\t\t\t\t\"Loading...\",\n304\t\t\t)\n305\t\t\t// Calculate height for loading view area, leave space for footer\n306\t\t\tloadingViewHeight := max(mm.Height-lipgloss.Height(styledFooter), 0)\n307\t\t\tcenteredLoadingView := lipgloss.Place(mm.Width, loadingViewHeight, lipgloss.Center, lipgloss.Center, loadingViewContent)\n308\t\t\treturn lipgloss.JoinVertical(lipgloss.Left, centeredLoadingView, styledFooter)\n309\t\t}\n310\t\n311\t\t// If not loading and no error, show the current view with logo and footer\n312\t\tcurrentViewRender := mm.CurrentView.View()\n313\t\n314\t\t// Join logo and current view horizontally\n315\t\tlogoAndCurrentView := lipgloss.JoinHorizontal(lipgloss.Top, logo, currentViewRender)\n316\t\n317\t\tfinalLayout := lipgloss.JoinVertical(lipgloss.Left, logoAndCurrentView, styledFooter)\n318\t\n319\t\treturn finalLayout\n320\t}\n321\t\n322\tfunc Run() {\n323\t\tmodel := InitialModel()\n324\t\tp := tea.NewProgram(model, tea.WithAltScreen())\n325\t\tif _, err := p.Run(); err != nil {\n326\t\t\tfmt.Printf(\"Alas, there's been an error: %v\", err)\n327\t\t\tos.Exit(1)\n328\t\t}\n329\t}\n330\t========== internal/latest/latest.go ==========\n331\tpackage latest\n332\t\n333\timport (\n334\t\t\"fmt\" // Added for View() title\n335\t\t\"html\"\n336\t\t\"strings\"\n337\t\n338\t\t\"github.com/charmbracelet/bubbles/list\"\n339\t\ttea \"github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea\"\n340\t\n341\t\t\"github.com/matt-riley/hopcli/internal/commands\"\n342\t)\n343\t\n344\ttype LatestModel struct {\n345\t\tproducts    *commands.Products\n346\t\tChoices     list.Model // Exported\n347\t\tCurrentPage int        // New\n348\t\tPerPage     int        // New\n349\t\tTotalItems  int        // New\n350\t\tTotalPages  int        // New\n351\t\t// width, height if needed for page change messages, or get from MainModel\n352\t}\n353\ttype LatestListItem struct {\n354\t\ttitle   string\n355\t\tdesc    string\n356\t\tbrewery string     { return i.brewery }\n362\tfunc (i LatestListItem) FilterValue() string { return i.title }\n363\t\n364\tfunc NewLatestModel() LatestModel {\n365\t\titems := []list.Item{}\n366\t\tl := list.New(items, list.NewDefaultDelegate(), 0, 0)\n367\t\tl.SetShowPagination(true) // Show list's pagination\n368\t\tl.SetShowStatusBar(true)  // Ensure status bar is shown for pagination page\n374\t\t}\n375\t}\n376\t\n377\tfunc (lm LatestModel) Init() tea.Cmd {\n378\t\treturn nil\n379\t}\n380\t\n381\tfunc (lm LatestModel) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n382\t\tvar cmd `\n⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢀⣰⣶⣶⣤⣤⣤⣤⣤⣀⠀⣀⣀⣀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀\n⠀⠀⠀⣀⣤⣤⣤⣬⣽⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣶⣄⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀\n⠀⠐⠚⣻⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⢿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣷⣄⣄⠀⠀⠀\n⠐⣶⣾⣿⣿⣿⢿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣦⣍⠻⣿⣿⣿⣿⣦⡙⢿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣷⡄⠀\n⠀⢿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣶⣍⠛⢿⣿⣿⣿⣷⡈⠻⣿⣿⣿⣿⡄⠹⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣷⡀\n⢠⣌⣿⣿⣿⡟⠁⠀⠀⠀⠈⠉⠛⠛⠛⠂⠈⠛⠋⠉⠁⠀⠀⠀⠙⣱⣿⣿⣿⡇\n⠘⣿⣿⣿⣿⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⣿⣿⣿⡿⠁\n⠀⢹⣿⣿⡟⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢻⣿⣿⠇⠀\n⠀⠀⢻⣿⣀⣠⠤⠤⠤⠤⠤⠤⣄⡀⠀⠀⠀⣀⡤⠤⠤⠤⠤⢤⣀⣈⣿⡟⠀⠀\n⠠⡖⢸⡟⡟⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢹⡶⢶⡏⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠈⣿⢻⡇⢾⡄\n⠀⡇⠀⡇⠰⡀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢠⠟⠀⠈⢣⡀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢠⠏⢸⠁⢸⠃\n⠀⠱⠀⡇⠀⠙⠢⠤⠤⠤⠤⠖⠋⠀⠀⠀⠀⠙⠢⠤⠤⠤⠤⠴⠋⠀⣼⠀⠃⠀\n⠀⠀⠀⣿⡀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢸⡏⠀⠀⠀\n⠀⠀⠀⣿⣧⠀⠀⠀⠀⢀⣠⣴⣶⡷⠶⠶⢶⣶⣦⣤⣀⠀⠀⠀⠀⣾⡇⠀⠀⠀\n⠀⠀⠀⠘⣿⣦⠀⠀⣴⡟⠉⠉⠁⢀⣀⣀⡀⠀⠈⠉⠻⣧⡀⢀⣼⣿⡇⠀⠀⠀\n⠀⠀⠀⠀⢸⣿⣷⣼⣿⣤⣤⣤⣴⣿⣿⣿⣿⣦⣤⣤⣤⣿⣷⣼⣿⣿⠃⠀⠀⠀\n⠀⠀⠀⠀⢸⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⡿⢿⣿⣿⣿⣿⡿⢿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⣿⡇⠀⠀⠀⠀\n⠀⠀⠀⠀⢼⣿⣿⡿⠿⠛⣉⣴⡈⢿⣿⣿⡿⢡⣦⣙⠻⠿⢿⣿⣿⡇⠀⠀⠀⠀\n⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⣶⣶⣶⠀⣿⣿⣿⣿⣦⠙⢋⣴⣿⣿⣿⣿⠀⣶⣶⡦⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀\n⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢿⣿⡿⠀⠿⠿⠿⢛⣡⣶⣷⣌⠛⠿⠿⠿⠀⣿⣿⡟⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀\n⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢰⣶⣶⣶⣷⠘⣿⣿⣿⡿⢁⣿⣶⣶⣶⡆⠈⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀\n⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠈⢿⣿⣿⡿⠓⣈⡛⢛⣁⠺⢿⣿⣿⡿⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀\n⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠉⠀⠐⢿⣿⣿⣿⣿⡗⠀⠈⠉⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀\n⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠈⠙⠿⠟⠋⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀`\n\ntype SessionState uint\n\nconst (\n\tDefaultView SessionState = iota\n\tLatestView\n\tCategoriesView\n\tCategoryProductsView\n\tProductView\n)\n\ntype MainModel struct {\n\tState                 SessionState\n\tCurrentView           tea.Model\n\tPreviousViews         []tea.Model // Stack to store previous views for back navigation\n\tDefaultModel          defaultview.DefaultModel\n\tLatestModel           latest.LatestModel\n\tCategoriesModel       categories.Model\n\tCategoryProductsModel categoryproducts.Model\n\tProductModel          productview.ProductModel\n\tLoading               bool\n\tSpinner               spinner.Model\n\tWidth                 int\n\tHeight                int\n\tErrMsg                string // To store and display error messages\n}\n\nfunc InitialModel() MainModel {\n\ts := spinner.New()\n\ts.Spinner = spinner.Dot\n\ts.Style = lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"205\"))\n\n\tdm := defaultview.InitialModel()\n\tlm := latest.NewLatestModel()\n\tcm := categories.NewCategoriesModel()\n\t// For cpm, categoryName and categoryID will be updated when a category is chosen.\n\t// Initialize with placeholder values.\n\tcpm := categoryproducts.NewModel(\"\", 0, \"\") // Added empty apiEndpoint\n\tpm := productview.NewProductModel()\n\n\treturn MainModel{\n\t\tState:                 DefaultView,\n\t\tCurrentView:           dm,\n\t\tDefaultModel:          dm,\n\t\tLatestModel:           lm,\n\t\tCategoriesModel:       cm,\n\t\tCategoryProductsModel: cpm,\n\t\tProductModel:          pm,\n\t\tPreviousViews:         []tea.Model{},\n\t\tLoading:               false,\n\t\tSpinner:               s,\n\t\t// Width and Height will be set by tea.WindowSizeMsg\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (mm MainModel) Init() tea.Cmd {\n\treturn mm.Spinner.Tick\n}\n\nfunc (mm MainModel) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tvar cmd tea.Cmd\n\tvar cmds []tea.Cmd\n\tvar updatedViewModel tea.Model\n\n\tswitch msg := msg.(type) {\n\tcase tea.WindowSizeMsg:\n\t\tmm.Width = msg.Width\n\t\tmm.Height = msg.Height\n\tcase tea.KeyMsg:\n\t\tswitch msg.String() {\n\t\tcase \"q\":\n\t\t\treturn mm, tea.Quit\n\t\tcase \"h\", \"left\":\n\t\t\tif len(mm.PreviousViews) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\tlastViewIndex := len(mm.PreviousViews) - 1\n\t\t\t\tmm.CurrentView = mm.PreviousViews[lastViewIndex]\n\t\t\t\tmm.PreviousViews = mm.PreviousViews[:lastViewIndex]\n\n\t\t\t\tswitch mm.CurrentView.(type) {\n\t\t\t\tcase defaultview.DefaultModel:\n\t\t\t\t\tmm.State = DefaultView\n\t\t\t\tcase latest.LatestModel:\n\t\t\t\t\tmm.State = LatestView\n\t\t\t\tcase categories.Model:\n\t\t\t\t\tmm.State = CategoriesView\n\t\t\t\tcase categoryproducts.Model:\n\t\t\t\t\tmm.State = CategoryProductsView\n\t\t\t\tcase productview.ProductModel:\n\t\t\t\t\tmm.State = ProductView\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\tcase defaultview.StartLoadingLatestMsg:\n\t\tmm.Loading = true\n\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n\t\t// Ensure initial load is page 1, use PerPage from the model (defaulted in NewLatestModel)\n\t\tcmds = append(cmds, commands.HandleGetLatest(mm.Width, mm.Height, 1, mm.LatestModel.PerPage))\n\n\tcase defaultview.StartLoadingCategoriesMsg:\n\t\tmm.Loading = true\n\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n\t\tcmds = append(cmds, commands.HandleGetCategories(mm.Width, mm.Height))\n\n\tcase commands.LatestResponseMsg:\n\t\tmm.Loading = false\n\t\tif msg.Err != nil {\n\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = msg.Err.Error()\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t// If not already in LatestView, it's an initial load for this view type.\n\t\t\tif mm.State != LatestView {\n\t\t\t\t// mm.LatestModel = latest.NewLatestModel() // Already initialized in InitialModel\n\t\t\t\tmm.PreviousViews = append(mm.PreviousViews, mm.CurrentView) // Only add to stack if changing view type\n\t\t\t\tmm.State = LatestView\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// Update the existing (or newly created) LatestModel instance\n\t\t\tupdatedViewModel, cmd = mm.LatestModel.Update(msg)\n\t\t\tmm.LatestModel = updatedViewModel.(latest.LatestModel)\n\t\t\tcmds = append(cmds, cmd)\n\t\t\tmm.CurrentView = mm.LatestModel // Ensure CurrentView points to the updated model\n\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n\t\t}\n\n\tcase commands.LoadLatestPageMsg: // New\n\t\tmm.Loading = true\n\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n\t\t// The CurrentView is still LatestModel, so we don't push to PreviousViews\n\t\t// We are just re-loading data for the current view type.\n\t\tcmds = append(cmds, commands.HandleGetLatest(mm.Width, mm.Height, msg.Page, msg.PerPage))\n\n\tcase commands.CategoriesResponseMsg:\n\t\tmm.Loading = false\n\t\tif msg.Err != nil {\n\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = msg.Err.Error()\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tupdatedViewModel, cmd = mm.CategoriesModel.Update(msg)\n\t\t\tmm.CategoriesModel = updatedViewModel.(categories.Model)\n\t\t\tcmds = append(cmds, cmd)\n\t\t\tmm.PreviousViews = append(mm.PreviousViews, mm.CurrentView)\n\t\t\tmm.CurrentView = mm.CategoriesModel\n\t\t\tmm.State = CategoriesView\n\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n\t\t}\n\n\tcase commands.StartLoadingProductsForCategoryMsg:\n\t\tmm.Loading = true\n\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n\t\t// Ensure initial load is page 1, use PerPage from the model (defaulted in NewCategoryProductsModel)\n\t\tcmds = append(cmds, commands.HandleGetProductsByCategory(mm.Width, mm.Height, msg.CategoryID, msg.CategoryName, msg.APIEndpoint, 1, mm.CategoryProductsModel.PerPage))\n\n\tcase commands.ProductsForCategoryResponseMsg:\n\t\tmm.Loading = false\n\t\tif msg.Err != nil {\n\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = msg.Err.Error()\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t// If current state is not CategoryProductsView OR if the category ID differs,\n\t\t\t// it's a new category product listing.\n\t\t\tif mm.State != CategoryProductsView || mm.CategoryProductsModel.CategoryID() != msg.CategoryID { // Used getter\n\t\t\t\tmm.CategoryProductsModel = categoryproducts.NewModel(msg.CategoryName, msg.CategoryID, msg.APIEndpoint)\n\t\t\t\tmm.PreviousViews = append(mm.PreviousViews, mm.CurrentView)\n\t\t\t\tmm.State = CategoryProductsView\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// Update the model (either newly created or existing)\n\t\t\tupdatedViewModel, cmd := mm.CategoryProductsModel.Update(msg)\n\t\t\tmm.CategoryProductsModel = updatedViewModel.(categoryproducts.Model)\n\t\t\tcmds = append(cmds, cmd)\n\t\t\tmm.CurrentView = mm.CategoryProductsModel\n\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n\t\t}\n\n\tcase commands.LoadCategoryProductsPageMsg: // New\n\t\tmm.Loading = true\n\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n\t\t// Similar to LoadLatestPageMsg, CurrentView is CategoryProductsModel.\n\t\tcmds = append(cmds, commands.HandleGetProductsByCategory(mm.Width, mm.Height, msg.CategoryID, msg.CategoryName, msg.APIEndpoint, msg.Page, msg.PerPage))\n\n\tcase commands.ProductsMsg: // This is for displaying a single product\n\t\tmm.Loading = false\n\t\tif msg.Err != nil {\n\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = msg.Err.Error()\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tupdatedViewModel, cmd = mm.ProductModel.Update(msg)\n\t\t\tmm.ProductModel = updatedViewModel.(productview.ProductModel)\n\t\t\tcmds = append(cmds, cmd)\n\t\t\tmm.PreviousViews = append(mm.PreviousViews, mm.CurrentView)\n\t\t\tmm.CurrentView = mm.ProductModel\n\t\t\tmm.State = ProductView\n\t\t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Update current view & spinner if loading\n\t// Only pass non-nil messages or messages the current view specifically handles\n\t// This avoids passing spinner ticks or other messages to views not expecting them if not loading\n\tif !mm.Loading {\n\t\tvar currentViewCmd tea.Cmd\n\t\tmm.CurrentView, currentViewCmd = mm.CurrentView.Update(msg)\n\t\tcmds = append(cmds, currentViewCmd)\n\t}\n\n\tif mm.Loading {\n\t\tvar spinCmd tea.Cmd\n\t\tmm.Spinner, spinCmd = mm.Spinner.Update(msg) // Spinner needs ticks\n\t\tcmds = append(cmds, spinCmd)\n\t} else {\n\t\t// If not loading, ensure current view is updated even if no specific msg type matched above\n\t\t// This handles general key presses for list navigation within the current view\n\t\tif _, ok := msg.(tea.KeyMsg); ok { // Only pass key messages if not loading and not handled above\n\t\t\tvar currentViewCmd tea.Cmd\n\t\t\tmm.CurrentView, currentViewCmd = mm.CurrentView.Update(msg)\n\t\t\tcmds = append(cmds, currentViewCmd)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn mm, tea.Batch(cmds...)\n}\n\nfunc (mm MainModel) View() string {\n\t// var mainViewContent string // Removed as it was declared and not used before re-assignment\n\tvar helpContent string\n\n\tswitch mm.State {\n\tcase DefaultView:\n\t\thelpContent = \"↑/↓: navigate | enter: select | q: quit\"\n\tcase LatestView, CategoryProductsView:\n\t\thelpContent = \"↑/↓: navigate | enter: select | h/←: back | n: next | p: prev | q: quit\"\n\tcase CategoriesView: // Categories view does not have n/p for its own list\n\t\thelpContent = \"↑/↓: navigate | enter: select | h/←: back | q: quit\"\n\tcase ProductView:\n\t\thelpContent = \"h/←: back | q: quit\"\n\tdefault:\n\t\thelpContent = \"q: quit\"\n\t}\n\n\tif mm.ErrMsg != \"\" {\n\t\thelpContent = \"h/←: back | q: quit\"\n\t}\n\n\tfooterStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().\n\t\tBorder(lipgloss.NormalBorder(), true, false, false, false).\n\t\tPaddingTop(1).\n\t\tForeground(lipgloss.Color(\"240\"))\n\n\tstyledFooter := footerStyle.Width(mm.Width).Render(helpContent)\n\n\tif mm.ErrMsg != \"\" {\n\t\terrorStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"#FF0000\")).Width(mm.Width).Align(lipgloss.Center)\n\t\terrorMessage := errorStyle.Render(\"Error: \" + mm.ErrMsg)\n\t\terrorViewHeight := max(mm.Height-lipgloss.Height(styledFooter), 0)\n\t\tcenteredError := lipgloss.Place(mm.Width, errorViewHeight, lipgloss.Center, lipgloss.Center, errorMessage)\n\t\treturn lipgloss.JoinVertical(lipgloss.Left, centeredError, styledFooter)\n\t}\n\n\tif mm.Loading {\n\t\tloadingViewContent := lipgloss.JoinVertical(\n\t\t\tlipgloss.Center,\n\t\t\tlogo,\n\t\t\tmm.Spinner.View(),\n\t\t\t\"Loading...\",\n\t\t)\n\t\t// Calculate height for loading view area, leave space for footer\n\t\tloadingViewHeight := max(mm.Height-lipgloss.Height(styledFooter), 0)\n\t\tcenteredLoadingView := lipgloss.Place(mm.Width, loadingViewHeight, lipgloss.Center, lipgloss.Center, loadingViewContent)\n\t\treturn lipgloss.JoinVertical(lipgloss.Left, centeredLoadingView, styledFooter)\n\t}\n\n\t// If not loading and no error, show the current view with logo and footer\n\tcurrentViewRender := mm.CurrentView.View()\n\n\t// Join logo and current view horizontally\n\tlogoAndCurrentView := lipgloss.JoinHorizontal(lipgloss.Top, logo, currentViewRender)\n\n\tfinalLayout := lipgloss.JoinVertical(lipgloss.Left, logoAndCurrentView, styledFooter)\n\n\treturn finalLayout\n}\n\nfunc Run() {\n\tmodel := InitialModel()\n\tp := tea.NewProgram(model, tea.WithAltScreen())\n\tif _, err := p.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"Alas, there's been an error: %v\", err)\n\t\tos.Exit(1)\n\t}\n}\n========== internal/latest/latest.go ==========\npackage latest\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\" // Added for View() title\n\t\"html\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/charmbracelet/bubbles/list\"\n\ttea \"github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea\"\n\n\t\"github.com/matt-riley/hopcli/internal/commands\"\n)\n\ntype LatestModel struct {\n\tproducts    *commands.Products\n\tChoices     list.Model // Exported\n\tCurrentPage int        // New\n\tPerPage     int        // New\n\tTotalItems  int        // New\n\tTotalPages  int        // New\n\t// width, height if needed for page change messages, or get from MainModel\n}\ntype LatestListItem struct {\n\ttitle   string\n\tdesc    string\n\tbrewery string\n}\n\nfunc (i LatestListItem) Title() string       { return i.title }\nfunc (i LatestListItem) Description() string { return i.desc }\nfunc (i LatestListItem) Brewery() string     { return i.brewery }\nfunc (i LatestListItem) FilterValue() string { return i.title }\n\nfunc NewLatestModel() LatestModel {\n\titems := []list.Item{}\n\tl := list.New(items, list.NewDefaultDelegate(), 0, 0)\n\tl.SetShowPagination(true) // Show list's pagination\n\tl.SetShowStatusBar(true)  // Ensure status bar is shown for pagination info\n\n\treturn LatestModel{\n\t\tChoices:     l, // Use exported field\n\t\tCurrentPage: 1,\n\t\tPerPage:     10, // Default items per page\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (lm LatestModel) Init() tea.Cmd {\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc (lm LatestModel) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tvar cmd tea.Cmd\n\tvar cmds []tea.Cmd // Declare cmds here\n\tswitch msg := msg.(type) {\n\tcase tea.KeyMsg:\n\t\tswitch msg.String() {\n\t\tcase \"enter\", \"return\":\n\t\t\tif len(lm.Choices.VisibleItems()) > 0 && lm.Choices.Index() < len(*lm.products) { // Use exported field, Check bounds\n\t\t\t\treturn lm, commands.HandleDisplayProduct(lm.Choices.Width(), lm.Choices.Height(), (*lm.products)[lm.Choices.Index()]) // Use exported field\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"n\": // Next page\n\t\t\tif lm.CurrentPage < lm.TotalPages {\n\t\t\t\tlm.CurrentPage++\n\t\t\t\t// Return a command to load the new page\n\t\t\t\treturn lm, func() tea.Msg {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn commands.LoadLatestPageMsg{Page: lm.CurrentPage, PerPage: lm.PerPage}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"p\": // Previous page\n\t\t\tif lm.CurrentPage > 1 {\n\t\t\t\tlm.CurrentPage--\n\t\t\t\t// Return a command to load the new page\n\t\t\t\treturn lm, func() tea.Msg {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn commands.LoadLatestPageMsg{Page: lm.CurrentPage, PerPage: lm.PerPage}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\tcase commands.LatestResponseMsg:\n\t\tif msg.Products == nil {\n\t\t\treturn lm, cmd // cmd might be nil here, which is fine\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tlm.products = msg.Products\n\t\tlm.TotalItems = msg.TotalItems // Store TotalItems\n\t\tlm.TotalPages = msg.TotalPages // Store TotalPages\n\n\t\tvar items []list.Item\n\t\tfor _, product := range *msg.Products {\n\t\t\tunescapedTitle := html.UnescapeString(product.Title.Rendered)\n\t\t\tbrewery := unescapedTitle // Assuming brewery is derived from title for now\n\n\t\t\tprocessedDesc := html.UnescapeString(product.Description.Rendered)\n\t\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(processedDesc, \"%%%\") {\n\t\t\t\tprocessedDesc = processedDesc[3:]\n\t\t\t} else if strings.HasPrefix(processedDesc, \"%%\") { // Handle cases with \"%%\"\n\t\t\t\tprocessedDesc = processedDesc[2:]\n\t\t\t} else if strings.HasPrefix(processedDesc, \"%\") { // Handle cases with \"%\"\n\t\t\t\tprocessedDesc = processedDesc[1:]\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Optional: Truncate if too long, even after stripping prefixes\n\t\t\tif len(processedDesc) > 150 { // Example max length for description\n\t\t\t\tprocessedDesc = processedDesc[:150] + \"...\"\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\titems = append(items, LatestListItem{title: unescapedTitle, desc: processedDesc, brewery: brewery})\n\t\t}\n\t\tlm.Choices.SetSize(msg.Width, msg.Height) // Use exported field\n\t\tlm.Choices.SetItems(items)                // Use exported field\n\n\t\t// Update list paginator\n\t\tlm.Choices.Paginator.PerPage = lm.PerPage      // Use exported field\n\t\tlm.Choices.Paginator.Page = lm.CurrentPage - 1 // list.Paginator is 0-indexed\n\t\tlm.Choices.Paginator.TotalPages = lm.TotalPages\n\n\t\t// No explicit cmd needed here unless SessionState uint\n\nconst (\n\tDefaultView SessionState = iota\n\tLatestView\n\tCategoriesView\n\tCategoryProductsView\n\tProductView\n)\n\ntype MainModel struct {\n\tState
